# Proofloop — complete historical phase and subphase audit

> Historical findings below are preserved. **Owner update, 9 October 2026:** Phase 8.2 is retired with no recovered scope; R82-01 is no longer an active blocker. Responsibility Phase 8 is COMPLETE for its defined verified scope. [Owner disposition](phase82-owner-retirement-2026-10-09.md) and the [active Phase 9 checkpoint](../benchmarks/phase9-vertical-slice-checkpoint-2026-10-09.md) supersede the earlier roadmap hold. No Phase 8.2 implementation or testing is claimed.

Date: **9 October 2026 (Asia/Calcutta)**. Revised after recovery of original project chat history and current verification. Current uncommitted code is included; application behavior, frozen prompts/labels/schemas/configuration and runtime flags were preserved. No live providers, held-out reruns, production database queries, shadow or consequence execution occurred.

## Summary

**Responsibility Phases 1–7 are VERIFIED for their original accepted scope. The V5 Phase 8.1 evaluation remains verified.** Eight earlier missing-history/quality findings are resolved. The original frozen Product Spec V1 and all 52 VM identifiers, original initiating instructions, and actual Phase 6D DB/coverage stdout were recovered from saved project sessions. Current backend tests passed **1559/1559**; typecheck passed; focused Phase 6D tests passed **26/26** with **100% statements/branches/functions/lines** over the three core modules and maximum **CRAP 13**.

The detailed [Phase 1–7 verification](proofloop-phase1-7-verification-2026-10-09.md) and source-bound [receipt](../../output/proofloop-phase1-7-verification-2026-10-09/verification-state.json) supersede the earlier unresolved findings R1-09, R1-10, R5-12, R6A-08, R6B-14, R6D-18, R6D-20 and R7-15. The [prior audit snapshot](../../output/proofloop-phase1-7-verification-2026-10-09/prior-complete-phase-audit.md) retains the earlier evidence state.

- **Original Phase 8.2 remains NOT FOUND** in the searched sources; no scope or functional omission is invented. Unqualified Phase 9 start certification remains NO pending only R82-01, separate from the checkpoint's narrower technical V5 readiness YES.
- Search S3's original customer UI/handoff is absent; its backend handoff was accepted, but no explicit reassignment was recovered. It remains independent Search work.
- Original Phase 7 §§27–28 explicitly require wiring other families only when trustworthy detection exists. Six absent detector families are accepted production coverage boundaries; broader zero-consequence integration and five unsupported financial semantics remain later work.
- Broader verified-payout eligibility/replacement/exceptional reversal work remains legitimately reserved. Phases 1–7 verification does not certify deployed consequences or full UI/E2E rollout.

There are **341 traceability records** at the documented grouping granularity. Only the original Phase 8.2 scope placeholder remains unknown.

## Audit method and confidence boundary

Current code, runtime callers, tests and raw artifacts take precedence over completion prose. Git supplies historical snapshots; uncommitted implementation was also inspected. The extensively dirty tree was preserved. Relevant CONTEXT.md and ROADMAP.txt sections were cross-checked against actual services/routes/jobs.

The earlier audit inspected 241 reachable commits, HEAD ab03176 (21 September), and recovered October 7 reflog snapshots 9f6a614/87dbd98. This continuation recovered September 21–30 project history and corresponding archives: **249 JSONL files, 175 project sessions and 992 selected records**. Nineteen exact original tasks/specification/selected completion and safe tool-output messages were retained with SHA256, timestamp and original JSONL location. This is not an exhaustive search of all later chats.

Original requirements establish scope; actual tool stdout and retained artifacts establish historical runs. Fresh current checks establish current backend regression and scoped Phase 6D quality. The dedicated 11/11 PostgreSQL run is historical (29 September), not freshly repeated. Its current test matches the October 7 Git blob; no reconstructed September source-tree hash is claimed. Full current backend tests use the repository's test doubles; no live DB/provider/production verification is implied.

Relevant searches covered original phases/subphases, source acceptance, simulator boundaries, accepted deferrals, Search UI/rollout ownership, TODO/FIXME/NOT_YET_SAFE/skips and runtime callers. Generic product and WhatsApp roadmap numbering was kept separate. Frozen V5 hashes, dataset membership/overlap and receipt identities were recomputed without inference or held-out reruns; all 22 frozen source identities match.

Stable requirement IDs are audit IDs. A complete record certifies its bounded criterion, not deployed activation, all 52 VM rules or UI behavior based on backend tests. Phase 9 work packages remain a proposed decomposition of accepted broad ownership; no named historical 9.x definitions were invented.

They are not an entire browser booking/ride simulator. |

| Financial guard | responsibilityFinancialConsequences.js:59–137 locks case/validates finality/lineage; financialPolicy.js exhaustively classifies seven kinds | Final authority plus supported driver cancellation fare required; no automatic unsupported-family charges. |
| Reliability new-work gate | driver.ts online/offer acceptance + driverAssignment.js + scheduledOffers.js + driverDispatchCandidates.js + nearbyDrivers.js call shared newWorkEligibility/recheck | Default lookup is durable isDriverReliabilityBlocked; fake registration is not a production always-allow stub. Payout verification is absent from this shared guard. |
| Payout ordinary edit lock | driverFinance.js:69–86 checks outstandingFinalizedLiabilityPaise before changing UPI; driver.ts:1740 invokes it | Ordinary lock implemented and unit-tested; active/pending account lifecycle and exceptional replacement are not implemented. Concurrent liability-creation/edit serialization remains an unproved integration question, not a reproduced defect in this audit. |
| Jev production selection | responsibilityShadowAdjudication.js:245–275 defaults to createProductionResponsibilityAnalyst; responsibilityJevV5.js is a ready injectable adapter | Frozen V5 benchmark does not imply default production analyst switched or live dual-shadow enabled. |
| Search customer invocation | services/search modules and fixture CLI exist; no customer read-only NL endpoint or frontend caller of this new pipeline found | Backend S2–S4 service completion is narrower than original S3 UI/confirmed handoff acceptance. |

**Composed zero-consequence boundary:** backend/lib/jobs.js:11–19 runs finalization/reliability/finance even when the shadow sweep returns CONFIG_ERROR. A disabled/shadow model flag is not a global consequence kill switch. This does not establish that deployed consequences are active, nor that shadow output can authorize them. Phase 9 must isolate and prove the whole writer composition; that verification belongs to its explicitly planned scope. Deployment variables, production DB contents and real cases were not inspected.

### Hidden-work and false-completion checks

- No skipped/todo Responsibility/integration test declaration was found in the scoped search. Real DB tests are a separate opt-in suite; ordinary backend passing totals do not imply DB runs. Relevant retained real DB runs are cited independently.
- NOT_YET_SAFE is an explicit exhaustive fail-closed financial disposition, not an unimplemented function that silently succeeds. An unsupported case does not starve valid later sweep entries.
- Historical quality artifacts have overlapping mutation reports and source evolution. Existing COMPLETE statements were checked against raw artifacts; timeouts/equivalent mutants were not relabeled killed or added twice.
- A full simulator was deliberately excluded from initial infrastructure; Phase 9 production-like composition is still missing. No claim that collections of fakes satisfy the final A–L lifecycle/user-journey contract.
- Current broad repository TODOs (for example assisted multi-stop product work, unrelated document/notifications features and compliance product audits) were not automatically assigned to Responsibility Phase 9. Multi-stop is a Search/product backlog dependency, not an authorized new policy in this audit.
- No original evidence mandates production AI activation, broad financial incident policy, completed human-final calibration or automatic consequences before the zero-consequence Phase 9 experiment. Frozen synthetic safety does not establish those permissions.

## G. PROPOSED corrected roadmap — no new approvals

1. **Close historical start certification:** resolve original 8.2 scope/disposition; Phase 6D and the Phase 1–7 evidence gaps are closed. Preserve all frozen evidence/configuration and historical reports. Add future errata with source hashes instead of rewriting past signoffs.
2. **Specify Phase 9 acceptance using its approved broad ownership:** isolate actual runtime handlers/jobs, use existing clock/DB/fakes, add production-like lifecycle/UI/E2E/replay/rollback evidence and prove all operative writers excluded. R9 work packages are a proposed decomposition. No named historical 9.x scope is invented.
3. **Include real incident-family integration only inside agreed zero-consequence scope:** retain unsupported final financial dispositions until incident-specific authoritative policy exists; do not infer penalties from broad benchmark support.
4. **Resume independent Search/language work in its own queue:** explicit S3 UI carry item and S5/S7 ownership reconciliation; complete 50 pending eligible Luna stages, S6 original verifier/current annex audit, full development/calibration, execution freeze then once-only 98 held-out. Browser/selection/confirmation and language grounding/challenge remain separate required rollout evidence.
5. **Keep financial production rollout gated:** verified-payout new-work eligibility, active/pending replacement, genuine-account exception, unsupported-family financial semantics and exceptional corrective workflow require their reserved scopes before operative expansion. These are not automatic permissions to redesign policy during Phase 9.
6. **After Responsibility Phase 9, apply Proofloop to selected high-risk RCS systems**, then refine common interfaces, generalize agent integrations and publish only under separately authorized scope. Do not redo passed Phase 1–8 modules or restart the superseded API comparator merely to appear complete.
